{"batchcomplete":"","query":{"pages":{"68289087":{"pageid":68289087,"ns":0,"title":"Bellator 270","revisions":[{"slots":{"main":{"contentmodel":"wikitext","contentformat":"text/x-wiki","*":"{{short description|Bellator mixed martial arts event in 2021}}\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=Bellator 270: Queally vs. Pitbull 2\n|image=Bellator270.jpg\n|image_size=\n|promotion=[[Bellator MMA]]\n|date={{Start date|2021|11|5}}\n|venue=[[3Arena (Dublin)|3Arena]]\n|city=[[Dublin]], Ireland\n|previousevent=[[Bellator 269|Bellator 269: Fedor vs. Johnson]]\n|followingevent=[[Bellator 271|Bellator 271: Cyborg vs. Kavanagh]]\n}}\n\n'''Bellator 270: Queally vs. Pitbull 2''' (also known as '''''Bellator Dublin''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by [[Bellator MMA]] that took place on November 5, 2021 at [[3Arena (Dublin)|3Arena]] in [[Dublin]], Ireland. The event marked Bellator's first return to Irish soil since February 2020.{{Cite web|date=2021-07-21|title=Bellator zeroes in on James Gallagher vs. Patchy Mix grudge match in Ireland|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/07/bellator-ireland-james-gallagher-vs-patchy-mix|access-date=2021-07-21|website=MMA Junkie|language=en-US}}\n\n== Background ==\nA lightweight bout between #1 ranked [[Patricky Pitbull]] and #4 ranked [[Peter Queally (fighter)|Peter Queally]] served as the headliner for this event. This was a rematch of their first bout that was stopped by the doctor due to a cut on Patricky at [[Bellator 258]].{{Cite web|date=2021-08-24|title='Bellator 270: Gallagher vs. Mix' official for Nov. 5 in Dublin, adds 'Pitbull' vs. Queally rematch|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/08/bellator-270-james-gallagher-vs-patchy-mix-official-for-dublin-adds-patricky-pitbull-vs-peter-queally|access-date=2021-08-24|website=MMA Junkie|language=en-US}} Originally set to be the co-headliner, the bout was promoted to the main event on October 6, 2021, when Patricky Pitbull's younger brother and [[List of Bellator MMA champions#Lightweight World Championship|Bellator MMA Lightweight World Champion]] [[Patr\u00edcio Pitbull]] announced he was vacating the title, not wanting to get in the way of his older brothers title aspirations. Patricky Pitbull vs. Peter Queally was for the vacant Bellator Lightweight World Championship.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/10/6/22712535/patricio-pitbull-vacates-lightweight-title-patricky-pitbull-peter-queally-title-fight-bellator-270|title=Patricio Pitbull vacates lightweight title; Peter Queally vs. Patricky Pitbull elevated to title fight at Bellator 270|work=MMAFighting.com|date=October 6, 2021|author=Mike Heck|access-date=October 6, 2021}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[Patchy Mix]] and James Gallagher served as the co-headliner for this event.{{Cite web|last=Lee|first=Alexander K.|date=2021-08-24|title=Bellator announces return to Dublin headlined by James Gallagher vs. Patchy Mix|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/8/24/22639302/bellator-announces-return-to-dublin-headlined-by-james-gallagher-vs-patchy-mix|access-date=2021-08-25|website=MMA Fighting|language=en}} The first scheduled booking between the two 135-pound contenders was supposed to take place at Bellator 258 on May 7, 2021. However, Gallagher withdrew from the bout for undisclosed reasons. Mix instead faced UFC alum [[Albert Morales]], whom he defeated via third-round submission.{{Cite web|last=Balfe|first=John|date=2021-07-21|title=James Gallagher vs. Patchy Mix being eyed for Bellator's return to Dublin \u2014 between former featherweight title challengers #6 ranked [[Daniel Weichel]] and #7 ranked [[Pedro Carvalho (fighter)|Pedro Carvalho]], and a middleweight bout between #6 ranked [[Charlie Ward (fighter)|Charlie Ward]] and #9 ranked [[Norbert N\u00f6v\u00e9nyi Jr.]] would appear on the main card as well.{{Cite web|last=Heck|first=Mike|date=2021-09-02|title=Daniel Weichel vs. Pedro Carvalho, Fighting|language=en}} On October 6, Norbert announced that he had suffered a torn ACL and had to pull out of the bout and was replaced by #4 ranked [[Fabian Edwards]].{{Cite web|date=2021-10-26|title=With Norbert Novenyi Jr. out, Fabian Edwards steps in to face Charlie Ward at Bellator however due to a ruptured bicep and was replaced by promotional newcomer Robert Fonseca.{{Cite web|last=Andy Stevenson|date=2021-10-28|title=Fabian Edwards vs Robert Fonseca Set for Bellator 270 in Dublin|url=https://mymmanews.com/fabian-edwards-vs-robert-fonseca-set-for-bellator-270-in-dublin/|access-date=2021-10-28|website=MyMMANews.com|language=en-US}} Fonseca couldn't get paperwork sorted in time and the bout was scratched, with no replacement sought. [[Ilias Bulaid]] vs. Georges Sasu was moved to the main card.{{Cite web|last=Anderson|first=Jay|date=2021-11-03|title=Fabian Edwards vs. Robert Fonseca Off Bellator 270 Card|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2021/11/03/fabian-edwards-vs-robert-fonseca-off-bellator-270-card/|access-date=2021-11-03|website=Cageside Press|language=en-US}}\n\nA lightweight bout between Darragh Kelly and Junior Morgan was scheduled for this event, however Morgan fell the night of the event and concussed himself, leading to the bout being scrapped.{{Cite web|last=Mooney|first=James Roulston|date=2021-11-05|title=Bellator 270 - Darragh Kelly's pro debut off after opponent is injured|url=https://www.sportsnewsireland.com/other_sports/bellator-270-darragh-kellys-pro-debut-off-after-opponent-is-injured/|access-date=2021-11-05|website=SportsNewsIreland|language=en-GB}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Patchy Mix]] missed weight for his bout, weighing in at 137.8 pounds, 1.7 pounds over the bantamweight non-title fight limit. The bout proceeded at catchweight and Mix was fined a percentage of his purse which went to his opponent James Gallagher.{{Cite web|date=2021-11-04|title=Bellator 270 weigh-in results: One miss on main card, but Queally-Freire title fight 2]]\n|followingevent=[[Bellator 272|Bellator 272: Pettis vs. Horiguchi]]\n}}\n\n'''Bellator 271: Cyborg vs. Kavanagh''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by [[Bellator MMA]] that took place on November 12, 2021, at the [[Seminole Hard Rock Hotel & Casino Hollywood]] in [[Hollywood, Florida]], United States.{{Cite web|last=Heck|first=Mike|date=2021-10-06|title=Cris Cyborg vs. Sinead Kavanagh featherweight title fight set for Bellator 271|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/10/6/22712516/cris-cyborg-vs-sinead-kavanagh-featherweight-title-fight-set-for-bellator-271|access-date=2021-10-06|website=MMA Fighting|language=en}}{{Cite web|date=2021-10-06|title=Cris Cyborg to defend title vs. Sinead Kavanagh at Bellator 271 on Nov. 12|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/10/bellator-271-cris-cyborg-to-defend-title-vs-sinead-kavanagh|access-date=2021-10-06|website=MMA Junkie|language=en-US}}\n\n== Background ==\nThe event was headlined by [[Cris Cyborg]] who faced off against [[Sinead Kavanagh]] for the [[List of Bellator MMA champions#Women's Featherweight World Championship|Bellator Women's Featherweight World Championship]]. Cyborg completed her third successful title defense since coming over from the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship|UFC]] in 2019. The 36-year-old captured the championship in her first promotional appearance when she stopped [[Julia Budd]] in the fourth round at Bellator 238 in January 2020. Since then, Cyborg has gone on to earn stoppage wins over [[Arlene Blencowe|Arlene Biencowe]] and [[Leslie Smith (fighter)|Leslie Smith]]. Kavanagh got her first shot at Bellator gold in her ninth promotional appearance. The SBG Ireland featherweight entered the bout on back-to-back victories \u2014 a unanimous decision win over [[Katharina Lehner]] at Bellator Euro Series 9 in October, and a second-round TKO of Olga Rubin at Bellator 234.\n\nA heavyweight bout between #6 ranked Steve Mowry and promotional newcomer Rakim Cleveland was also scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|date=2021-10-19|title=Heavyweights Steve Mowry, Rakim Cleveland set for Bellator 271 showdown|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/10/mma-news-bellator-271-steve-mowry-vs-rakim-cleveland|access-date=2021-10-19|website=MMA Junkie|language=en-US}}\n\nA middleweight bout between Jordan Newman and Shane O'Shea was scheduled for the event, however O'Shea won't weigh-in due to undisclosed reasons and the bout was scrapped.{{Cite web|date=2021-11-11|title=Bellator 271 weigh-in results: Cris Cyborg vs. Sinead Kavanagh official; one prelim canceled|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/11/bellator-271-cris-cyborg-sinead-kavanagh-weigh-in-results|access-date=2021-11-11|website=MMA Junkie|language=en-US}}\n\n== Results web|date=2021-10-20|title=Pettis to defend Bellator title against Fighting|language=en}}{{Cite web|date=2021-10-20|title=Sergio Pettis set to defend title vs. Kyoji Horiguchi at Bellator 272|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/10/mma-news-bellator-272-sergio-pettis-vs-kyoji-horiguchi|access-date=2021-10-20|website=MMA Junkie|language=en-US}}\n\n== Background ==\n[[Sergio Pettis]] made his first defence of the [[List of Bellator MMA champions#Bantamweight World Championship|Bellator Bantamweight World Championship]] after winning his first major promotional title in May when he defeated then-champ [[Juan Archuleta]] by unanimous decision in the main event of [[Bellator 258]]. [[Kyoji Horiguchi]], meanwhile, competed under the [[Bellator MMA|Bellator]] banner for the first time as a rostered fighter on a multifight deal. In June 2019, Horiguchi was lent by [[Rizin Fighting Federation|RIZIN]] to Bellator for a one-off fight against [[Darrion Caldwell]] \u2013 a bout Horiguchi won by unanimous decision to become champion. He later vacated the title after Horiguchi suffered a knee injury that forced him to relinquish the title while he was recovering from surgery.\n\nA featherweight bout between John de Jesus and Keoni Diggs was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|last=McDonagh|first=Joe|date=2021-10-27|title=Bellator 272 Adds Vinicius De Jesus vs. Levan Chokheli|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2021/10/27/bellator-272-vinicius-de-jesus-levan-chokheli/|access-date=2021-10-30|website=Cageside Press|language=en-US}} However, Diggs had to pull out for unknown reasons and was replaced by [[Kai Kamaka III]].{{Cite web|last=Behunin|first=Alex|date=2021-10-29|title=Kai Kamaka III's Bellator Debut Is Set, Fights John De Jesus On December 3 At Bellator 272|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2021/10/29/kai-kamaka-iiis-bellator-debut-set-fights-john-de-jesus-december-3-bellator-272/|access-date=2021-10-30|website=Cageside Press|language=en-US}}\n\nA lightweight bout between Dan Moret and Mandel Nallo was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|title=Mandel Nallo will be facing Dan Moret at Bellator 272 on December 3 in Uncasville, CT per sources.|url=https://twitter.com/aaronbronsteter/status/1454157972154818563|url-status=live|access-date=2021-10-30|website=Twitter|language=en|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20211029193152/https://twitter.com/aaronbronsteter/status/1454157972154818563 Davis, three other fights|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/11/mma-news-bellator-272-jay-jay-wilson-vs-alfie-davis-kyle-crutchmer-oliver-enkamp|access-date=2021-11-02|website=MMA web|date=2021-11-27|title=Spike Carlyle fills in on short notice to fight Dan Moret at Bellator and Alfie Davis was scheduled for this event, however Davis pulled out of the bout for unknown reasons.{{Cite web|last=Sherdog.com|title=Jay-Jay Wilson vs. Alfie Davis Off Bellator 272; Hill vs. Scoggins Elevated to Main Card|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/JayJay-Wilson-vs-Alfie-Davis-Off-Bellator-272-Hill-vs-Scoggins-Elevated-to-Main-Card-183277|access-date=2021-11-29|website=Sherdog}}{{Cite Background ==\nA winner in eight of his last 11 bouts, Bader will be making his return to the heavyweight division for the first time since 2019, Bader\u2019s last title defense came against the [[Cheick Kongo]] at [[Bellator MMA in 2019#Bellator 226|Bellator 226]], a bout Bader was controlling until an accidental eye poke prematurely ended the contest. Since then, Bader lost his 205-pound title against [[Vadim Nemkov]] before entering the [[Bellator MMA in 2021#Bellator Light Heavyweight World Grand Prix Tournament|light heavyweight grand prix]] where he defeated [[Lyoto Machida]] in April before suffering a KO loss to [[Corey Anderson (fighter)|Corey Anderson]] in October at [[Bellator 268]]. Moldavsky, a protege of [[Fedor Emelianenko]], became Bellator\u2019s first-ever interim heavyweight champion with a unanimous decision win over [[Timothy Johnson (fighter)|Timothy Johnson]] at [[Bellator 261]].\n\nA middleweight bout between Romero Cotton and Dalton Rosta was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|date=2021-12-20|title=Bellator 273 pins Romero Cotton against Dalton Rosta in prospect showcase|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/12/bellator-273-news-romero-cotton-vs-dalton-rosta-fight|access-date=2022-01-14|website=MMA Junkie|language=en-US}} However, two weeks before the event Cotton pulled out of the bout due to illness and was replaced by Duane Johnson.{{Cite web|title=Bellator middleweight Dalton Rosta (5-0) will now be facing Sanford MMA's Duane Johnson (6-2) at Bellator 273 on Jan. a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by [[Bellator MMA]] that took place on April 15, 2022, at the [[SAP Center]] in [[San Jose, California]], United States.{{Cite web|date=2022-02-16|title=AJ McKee to rematch Pitbull in first title defense at Bellator 277|url=https://www.ocregister.com/2022/02/16/aj-mckee-to-rematch-pitbull-in-first-title-defense-at-bellator-277|access-date=2022-02-16|website=Orange County Register|language=en-US}}{{Cite web|last=Cruz|first=Guilherme|date=2022-02-16|title=A.J. J. McKee]] and former two-time champion (also former [[Bellator Lightweight World Championship|Bellator Lightweight World Champion]]) [[Patr\u00edcio Pitbull]]. The duo previously fought in the [[Bellator 263]] main event, where McKee took Pitbull's title in the [[Bellator MMA in 2021#Bellator Featherweight World Grand Prix Tournament|Bellator Featherweight World Grand Prix]] final via a first-round guillotine choke submission.{{Cite web|title=Bellator 277 fight card: AJ McKee vs. Patricio Pitbull 2, Vadim Nemkov vs. Corey Anderson set for April|url=https://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/bellator-277-fight-card-aj-mckee-vs-patricio-pitbull-2-vadim-nemkov-vs-corey-anderson-set-for-april/|access-date=2022-02-16|website=CBSSports.com|language=en}}{{Cite Sports|language=en-US}}\n\nThe co-main event featured the final of the [[2022 in Bellator MMA#Bellator Light Heavyweight World Grand Prix Tournament|Bellator Light Heavyweight World Grand Prix Tournament]], as a [[List of Bellator MMA champions#Light Heavyweight World Championship|Bellator Light Heavyweight World Championship]] bout between current champion [[Vadim Nemkov]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Edgar vs. Team Penn]] light heavyweight winner [[Corey Anderson (fighter)|Corey Anderson]] as well as the $1 million prize.{{Cite web|date=2022-02-16|title=Vadim Nemkov vs. Corey Anderson light heavyweight grand prix final co-headlines Bellator 277|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/02/bellator-277-vadim-nemkov-vs-corey-anderson-light-heavyweight-grand-prix-final-booked|access-date=2022-02-16|website=MMA Junkie|language=en-US}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between former interim [[Bellator Heavyweight World Championship]] challenger [[Timothy Johnson (fighter)|Timothy Johnson]] and Tyrell Fortune was scheduled for the event.{{Cite |language=en}}\n\n== Background ==\nThe event marked the promotion's fifth visit to Hawaii and first since [[2019 in Bellator MMA#Bellator 236|Bellator 236]] in December 2019. The event was dedicated to military and first responders meaning that there would be no tickets on sale to the general public. \n\nThe event was headlined by a [[List of Bellator champions#Women's Flyweight World Championship|Bellator Women's Flyweight World Championship]] bout between undefeated current champion [[Juliana Velasquez]] and [[Liz Carmouche]].{{Cite web |date=2022-03-22 |title=Bellator 278 headliner set with Juliana Velasquez defending against Liz Carmouche |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/03/bellator-278-julianna-velasquez-vs-liz-carmouche-hawaii-troops |access-date=2022-03-22 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}}\n\nThe event also featured two wild card qualifier bouts for the [[2022 in Bellator MMA#Bellator Bantamweight World Grand Prix Tournament|Bellator Bantamweight World Grand Prix]] due to injuries removing [[List of Bellator MMA champions|Bellator Bantamweight World Champion]] [[Sergio Pettis]] and James Gallagher from the tournament. [[Josh Hill (fighter)|Josh Hill]] was to face [[Enrique Barzola]] with the winner later meeting Magomed Magomedov in the opening round, while the winner of the second qualifier between Jornel Lugo and [[Danny Sabatello]] would face [[Leandro Higo]] in the first round of the tournament.{{Cite web |date=2022-03-22 |title=Bellator adds two wild card bouts, four fighters to bantamweight grand prix |language=en}}\n\n== Background ==\n\nThe main event featured [[List of Bellator MMA champions#Women's Featherweight World Championship|Bellator Women's Featherweight Champion]] [[Cris Cyborg]] defending her title in a rematch against [[Arlene Blencowe]].\n\nAs the start of the $1-million [[2022 in Bellator MMA#Bellator Bantamweight World Grand Prix Tournament|Bellator Bantamweight World Grand Prix]], the opening round was originally set to start with reigning [[List of Bellator MMA champions#Bantamweight World Championship|Bellator Bantamweight Champion]] [[Sergio Pettis]] facing off against former teammate [[Raufeon Stots]], while former champion [[Kyoji Horiguchi]] faced [[Patchy Mix]]. However, Pettis was forced to pull out of the bout and the Grand Prix after sustaining an injury that required surgery, resulting in [[Juan Archuleta]] taking his place and the bout instead being held for the [[List of Bellator MMA champions#Bantamweight World Championship|Interim Bellator Bantamweight Championship]].{{Cite web |title=Bellator 279 card: Sergio Pettis out of Bantamweight World Grand Prix with injury, Juan Archuleta steps in |url=https://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/bellator-279-card-sergio-pettis-out-of-bantamweight-world-grand-prix-with-injury-juan-archuleta-steps-in/ |access-date=2022-03-22 |website=CBSSports.com |language=en}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, Whittany Pyles missed weight for her bout, weighing in at 127.4 pounds, 1.4 pounds over the flyweight non-title fight limit. The bout proceeded at [[catchweight]] and Pyles was fined a percentage of her purse which went to her opponent [[Sumiko Inaba]].{{Cite web |date=2022-04-22 |title=Bellator 279 weigh-in results: Title bout competitors on point, but one prelim fighter misses her mark |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/04/bellator-279-honolulu-hawaii-cris-cyborg-arlene-blencowe-ilima-lei-macfarlane-weigh-in-results |access-date=2022-04-23 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}}\n\n== Results ==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Showtime)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Women's Featherweight\n|[[Cris Cyborg]] (c)\n|def.\n|[[Arlene Blencowe]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (49\u201345, 49\u201345, 49\u201345)\n|5\n|5:00\n|For the [[Bellator Women's Featherweight World Championship]]. ==\nThe event marked the promotion's second visit to Paris and first since [[2020 in Bellator MMA#Bellator 248|Bellator 248]] in October 2020.\n\nA [[List of Bellator MMA champions#Heavyweight World Championship|Bellator Heavyweight World Championship]] rematch between current champion (also former [[List of Bellator champions#Light Heavyweight World Championship|Bellator Light Heavyweight World Champion]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Nogueira vs. Team Mir]] light heavyweight winner) [[Ryan Bader]] and [[Cheick Kongo]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|title=Bellator MMA {{!}} Bellator MMA Set for Return to Paris on Friday, May 6 With Massive Heavyweight World Championship Bout|url=https://www.bellator.com/article/Bader-Kongo-2-Set-for-may-6|access-date=2022-01-30|website=www.bellator.com|language=en-US}} The pairing previously met at [[Bellator MMA in 2019#Bellator 226|Bellator 226]] in 2019, which the bout ended due to an accidental poke to Kongo's left eye in the opening round, resulting in a no contest.{{Cite web |date=2019-09-08 |title=Bellator 226 results: Ryan Bader vs. Cheick Kongo ruled no contest after eye poke |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2019/09/bellator-226-results-ryan-bader-cheick-kongo-no-contest-eye-poke |access-date=2022-03-03 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}}\n\nA featherweight bout between [[Pedro Carvalho (fighter)|Pedro Carvalho]] and Khasan Askhabov was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web |date=2022-02-23 |title=Pedro Carvalho vs. Khasan Askhabov set for Bellator Paris on 6th May |url=http://severemma.com/2022/02/pedro-carvalho-vs-khasan-askhabov-set-for-bellator-paris-on-6th-may/ |access-date=2022-03-18 |website=SevereMMA.com - UFC, Mixed Martial Arts (MMA), Irish MMA |language=en-GB}} However, due to undisclosed reasons, Askhabov was forced to pull out and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Piotr Niedzielski]].{{Cite web |last=Andy Stevenson |date=2022-03-18 |title=Breaking: Pedro Carvalho vs Piotr Niedzielski Booked for Bellator 280 being for the interim title.{{Cite news |title=MVP bout off as Amosov fights Russia |language=en-GB |work=BBC Sport |url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/mixed-martial-arts/60840577 |title=Bellator 283 Announced for Tacoma, Washington with Patricky Pitbull vs. Sidney Outlaw Headlining Pitbull to take on Sydney Outlaw in Washington |url=https://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/bellator-283-fight-lightweight-champion-patricky-pitbull-to-take-on-sydney-outlaw-in-washington/ However on July 4, it was announced that Pitbull had sustained an injury and that the bout would be moved to a later date.{{cite web|last=Critchfield|first=Tristen|title=Patricky Freire Suffers |title=Ex-RIZIN champ Tofiq Musayev replaces Patricky Freire, as Bellator 283 elevates Douglas Lima vs. Jason Jackson The bout was initially scheduled for May 13, 2022 at [[Bellator 281]].{{Cite news |title=Lima added to Bellator London card |language=en-GB |work=BBC Sport |url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/mixed-martial-arts/60276649 |access-date=2022-02-08}} However, for unknown reasons, the bout was pulled from the event and was rescheduled for this event.{{Cite web |date=2022-04-17 |title=Bellator 281 loses Douglas Lima vs. Jason Jackson co-main event |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/04/bellator-281-loses-douglas-lima-vs-jason-jackson-co-main-event |access-date=2022-04-17 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}} After the main event between Pitbull and Outlaw was scrapped and the reshuffling of the bouts, the bout was promoted to new headliner and 5 rounds.\n\nA light heavyweight bout between [[Viktor Nemkov]] and Jos\u00e9 Augusto Azevedo was scheduled for this event, however due to an injury to Nemkov, the bout was scrapped.{{Cite web |last=Anderson |first=Jay |date=2022-07-05 |title=Patricky Pitbull Off Bellator 283, Card Gets New Main Event |url=https://cagesidepress.com/2022/07/05/patricky-pitbull-off-bellator-283-card-gets-new-main-event/ |access-date=2022-07-06 |website=Cageside Press |language=en-US}}\n\nA lightweight bout between Justin Montalvo and Vladimir Tokov was scheduled for this event, however due to an injury to Tokov, he pulled out of the bout and was replaced by Archie Colgan. Colgan instead faced Bryan Nuro in a 160 lb catchweight bout.{{cite web |title=Bellator MMA {{!}} EIGHT UNDEFEATED PROSPECTS SET FOR ACTION AT BELLATOR 283: LIMA VS. However in July, the bout was scrapped for unknown reasons.{{Cite web |title=Bellator 284 has lost Weber Almeida vs. Ilias Bulaid. Bout scheduled for Aug. 12 in Sioux Falls, S.D. has been canceled |url=https://twitter.com/mma_kings/status/1552726634280665089 |access-date=2022-07-28 |website=Twitter |language=en}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between Sarvadzhon Khamidov and Jared Scoggins was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web |last=Anderson |first=Jay |date=2022-06-27 |title=Sarvadzhon Khamidov Set to Debut Against Jared Scoggins at Bellator 284 |url=https://cagesidepress.com/2022/06/27/sarvadzhon-khamidov-set-debut-jared-scoggins-bellator-284/ |access-date=2022-08-02 |website=Cageside Press |language=en-US}} However, the bout was scrapped for unknown reasons\n\nA middleweight bout between [[Austin Vanderford]] and Anthony Adams was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web |last=King |first=Nolan |date=2022-07-12 |title=Bellator 284 adds Austin Vanderford vs. Anthony Adams |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/07/bellator-284-austin-vanderford-vs-anthony-adams-sioux-falls |access-date=2022-07-12 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}} However after Adams pulled out of the bout 2 weeks before the event, Aaron Jeffery stepped in on short notice.{{Cite web |last=Hannoun |first=Farah |date=2022-08-04 |title=With Anthony Adams out, Aaron Jeffery steps in to face Austin Vanderford at Bellator 284 |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/08/bellator-284-austin-vanderford-vs-aaron-jeffery-with-anthony-adams-out-injury-mma-fight-booking-matchup |access-date=2022-08-04 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}}\n\nAt weigh ins, three fighters missed weight; [[Ilima-Lei Macfarlane]], who was three pounds over the division non-title fight limit at 129 lbs, [[Justine Kish]] who came in at 128.4 lbs for their flyweight bout, and Nick Perez who came in at 157.4 lbs for his lightweight bout. Macfarlane and Kish were fined a percentage of their purses and the bouts continued at catchweights, while Perez's bout against Isaiah Hokit was scrapped.{{Cite web |date=2022-08-11 |title=Bellator 284 weigh-in results: Ilima-Lei Macfarlane, two others heavy; one fight canceled J. McKee]] and [[Spike Carlyle]].\n\nAt weigh ins, the bout between [[Juan Archuleta]] and [[Enrique Barzola]] was moved to a catchweight of 141 pounds; while two fighters missed weight; [[Spike Carlyle]], was .6 pounds over the division non-title fight limit at 156.6 lbs, and Dominic Clark, who came in at 2.2 pounds over the limit at 158.2 lbs for his lightweight bout. Heavyweight Among Mass Arrests In Bosnia |url=https://www.mmanews.com/2022/11/watch-former-ufc-heavyweight-among-mass-arrests-in-bosnia/ |access-date=2022-11-05 |website=MMA News |language=en-US}}\n\n==Results ==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Showtime)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|[[Mansour Barnaoui]]\n|def.\n|[[Adam Piccolotti]]\n|Submission (rear-naked was headlined by a rematch in the final of the [[2022 in Bellator MMA#Bellator Light Heavyweight World Grand Prix Tournament|Bellator Light Heavyweight World Grand Prix Tournament]] for the [[Bellator Light Heavyweight World Championship]] between the champion [[Vadim Nemkov]] and former title challenger [[Corey Anderson (fighter)|Corey Anderson]]. mixed martial arts event in 2022}}\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=Bellator 289: Stots vs. Sabatello\n|image=File:Bellator289.jpg\n|image_size=\n|promotion=[[Bellator MMA]]\n|date={{Start date|2022|12|9}}\n|venue=[[Mohegan Sun Arena]]\n|city=[[Uncasville, Connecticut]], United States\n|previousevent=[[Bellator 288|Bellator 288: Nemkov vs. Anderson 2]]\n|followingevent=[[Bellator MMA vs. Rizin|Bellator MMA vs. Rizin FF]]\n}}\n\n'''''Bellator 289: Stots vs. Sabatello''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by [[Bellator MMA]] that took place on December 9, 2022, at the [[Mohegan Sun Arena]] in [[Uncasville, Connecticut]], United States.\n\n== Background ==\nThe semi-finals of the $1-million [[2022 in Bellator MMA#Bellator Bantamweight World Grand Prix Tournament|Bellator Bantamweight World Grand Prix]] took place at this event, with interim champ [[Raufeon Stots]] taking on [[Danny Sabatello]], while [[Patchy Mix]] took on [[Magomed Magomedov (fighter)|Magomed Magomedov]]. Stots defeated [[Juan Archuleta]] for the [[List of Bellator MMA champions#Bantamweight World Championship|Interim Bellator Bantamweight Championship]] in their last outing, knocking him out by the way of headkick in the third round. At the same event, [[Patchy Mix]] defeated [[Kyoji Horiguchi]] by unanimous decision. Sabatello entered the tournament as a wild card, having defeated Jornel Lugo via unanimous decision for a spot in the tournament, while Magomed Magomedov defeated other wild card [[Enrique Barzola]] via guillotine choke in the fourth round.{{Cite web |date=2022-08-29 |title=Stots-Sabatello, Magomedov-Mix bantamweight semifinals set for Bellator 289 in December |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/08/bellator-289-bantamweight-tournament-semifinals-raufeon-stots-vs-danny-sabatello-magomed-magomedov-vs-patchy-mix |access-date=2022-08-29 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}}\n\nA title bout rematch for the [[List of Bellator champions#Women's Flyweight World Championship|Bellator Women's Flyweight World Championship]] between champion [[Liz Carmouche]] and [[Juliana Velasquez]] took place as the co-main event. Carmouche won the title from Velasquez in April; however, the TKO stoppage was considered controversial as many felt that it happened too early.{{Cite web |last=Cruz |first=Guilherme |date=2022-09-14 |title=Flyweight champion Liz Carmouche, Juliana Velasquez set to rematch at Bellator 289 |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/9/14/23352720/flyweight-champion-liz-carmouche-juliana-velasquez-rematch-bellator-289-december |access-date=2022-09-14 |website=MMA Fighting |language=en}} Following the bout, Velasquez's team appealed the result on the grounds of refereeing error made by Mike Beltran, but the appeal was denied by the Hawaii State Boxing Commission.{{cite web |author=Guilherme Cruz |date=July 5, 2022 |title=Juliana Velasquez appeal of Bellator 278 title loss to Liz Carmouche denied by Hawaii commission description|Bellator mixed martial arts event in 2023}}\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=Bellator 290: Bader vs. Fedor 2\n|image=Bellator290.jpg\n|image_size=\n|promotion=[[Bellator MMA]]\n|date={{Start date|2023|02|4}}\n|venue=[[Kia Forum]]\n|city=[[Inglewood, California]], United States\n|previousevent=[[Bellator MMA vs. Rizin|Bellator MMA vs. Rizin FF]]\n|followingevent=[[Bellator 291|Bellator 291: Amosov vs. Storley 2]]\n}}\n\n'''''Bellator 290: Bader vs. Fedor 2''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by [[Bellator MMA]] that took place on February 4, 2023, at the [[Kia Forum]] in [[Inglewood, California]], United States. The card aired live on [[CBS]] and [[Paramount Plus]], the first time the promotion has aired live on the network and the first time MMA has been broadcast live on CBS since 2010.\n\n== Background ==\nThe event marked the promotion's seventh visit to Inglewood and first since [[Bellator 263]] in July 2021.\n\nThe main event of the Bellator 290 featured MMA veteran and former [[List of Pride Fighting Championships champions#Heavyweight Championship|PRIDE Heavyweight Champion]] [[Fedor Emelianenko]]'s last fight before his retirement. Emelianenko challenged [[Bellator Heavyweight World Championship|Bellator Heavyweight champion]] [[Ryan Bader]] (also former [[Bellator Light Heavyweight World Championship|Bellator Light Heavyweight Champion]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Nogueira vs. Team Mir]] light heavyweight winner) for the title.{{cite web |title=Fedor Emelianenko to fight Ryan Bader for Bellator title in retirement bout |url=https://abcnews.go.com/Sports/fedor-emelianenko-fight-ryan-bader-bellator-title-retirement/story?id=93564354 |access-date=2022-11-18 |website=ABC News |language=en}} The fight, which was the first event of [[2023 in Bellator MMA|2023]] for the promotion, was a rematch of their January 2019 encounter in which Bader defeated his opponent by TKO in the first round to win the [[Bellator Heavyweight World Championship]].{{cite web |title=Bellator 290: Fedor Emelianenko set for retirement bout against Ryan Bader in February showdown on CBS |url=https://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/bellator-290-fedor-emelianenko-set-for-retirement-bout-against-ryan-bader-in-february-showdown-on-cbs/ |access-date=2022-11-18 |website=CBSSports.com |language=en}}\n\nThe event was shown live on [[CBS]] and [[Paramount Plus]], marking the first time the promotion has done so and the first time MMA has been broadcast live on the network since 2010.{{cite news |title=Bellator MMA to make CBS debut with Fedor-Bader showdown |language=en-US |newspaper=Washington Post |url=https://www.washingtonpost.com/sports/bellator-mma-to-make-cbs-debut-with-fedor-bader-showdown/2022/11/18/4b7062a2-6766-11ed-b08c-3ce222607059_story.html |access-date=2022-11-18 |issn=0190-8286}}\n\nA [[Bellator Light Heavyweight World Championship]] bout between current champion [[Vadim Nemkov]] (also the [[2022 in Bellator MMA#Bellator Light Heavyweight World Grand Prix Tournament|Bellator Light Heavyweight World Grand Prix Champion]]) and former UFC Middleweight title contender [[Yoel Romero]] was expected to take place at the event.{{cite web |last=Lee |first=Alexander K. |date=2022-11-18 |title=Yoel Romero to fight Vadim Nemkov vs. Corey Anderson 2 winner at Bellator 290 |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/11/18/23466360/yoel-romero-to-fight-vadim-nemkov-vs-corey-anderson-2-winner-at-bellator-290 |access-date=2022-11-21 |website=MMA Fighting |language=en}} However, Nemkov withdrew for undisclosed reasons and the bout was cancelled.{{cite promotion's 13th visit to San Jose and first since [[Bellator 277]] in April 2022.\n\nAs part of the $1 million [[2023 in Bellator MMA#Bellator Lightweight World Grand Prix Tournament|Bellator Lightweight World Grand Prix]], a [[Bellator Lightweight World Championship]] bout between current champion [[Usman Nurmagomedov]] and [[Benson Henderson]] (former [[List of World Extreme Cagefighting champions#Lightweight Championship|WEC]] and [[List of UFC champions#Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight Champion]]) headlined the event. In the co-main event, [[Tofiq Musayev]] faced off against [[Alexandr Shabliy]] in a quarterfinal match. After the main event bout, Henderson announced his retirement from professional mixed martial arts.{{Cite web |date=2023-03-10 |title=Usman Nurmagomedov Retains Title, Retires Benson Henderson at Bellator 292 |url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Usman-Nurmagomedov-Retains-Title-Retires-Benson-Henderson-at-Bellator-292-189379 |access-date=2023-03-10 |website=Sherdog |language=en-US}}{{Cite web |date=2023-03-10 |title= Bellator 292 results: Former UFC champ Benson Henderson retires after first-round loss to Usman Nurmagomedov |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/03/bellator-292-results-former-ufc-champ-benson-henderson-retires-after-first-round-loss-to-usman-nurmagomedov |access-date=2023-03-10 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between former title challenger [[Leandro Higo]] (also former [[Legacy Fighting Alliance#LFA Bantamweight Championship|LFA Bantamweight Champion]]) and James Gallagher was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web |last=Stevenson |first=Andy |date=2023-01-18 |title=James Gallagher vs Leandro Higo set for Bellator 292 |url=https://svr1.severemma.com/2023/01/james-gallagher-vs-leandro-higo-set-for-bellator-292/ |access-date=2023-01-18 |website=SevereMMA.com - It was also dedicated for military and first responders meaning that there will be no tickets on sale to the general public.\n\nA [[Bellator Women's Flyweight World Championship]] bout between current champion [[Liz Carmouche]] and [[DeAnna Bennett]] headlined the event. Bennett and Carmouche previously squared off in September 2020, with Carmouche submitting Bennett in both fighters' Bellator debuts.\n\nAt the weigh-ins, Bennett weighed in at 126.2 pounds, 1.2 pounds over the title flyweight fight limit. The bout proceeded at catchweight with Bennett being fined 30% of his purse, which went to her opponent Carmouche. Carmouche also chose to keep the title on the line, meaning if she loses the fight, the title will become vacant. Likewise, Blake Smith (146.4 lbs) and Cris Lencioni (152.4 lbs) both missed weight for their bout, coming in .4 and 6.4 pounds over the non-title limits. The bout proceeded at catchweight and both were fined a percentage of their purses.{{Cite web |last=Lee |first=Alexander K. |date=2023-04-20 |title=Bellator 294 weigh-in results: DeAnna Bennett misses for flyweight title fight, another fighter heavy by over 6 pounds |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/4/20/23691612/bellator-294-weigh-in-results-deanna-bennett-misses-flyweight-title-fight-another-heavy-by-6-pounds |access-date=2023-04-20 |website=MMA Fighting |language=en}}\n\n== Results ==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Showtime)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Women's Flyweight\n|[[Liz Carmouche]] (c)\n|def.\n|[[DeAnna Bennett]]\n|Submission (arm-triangle choke)\n|4\n|4:29\n|For the [[Bellator Women's Flyweight World Championship]]. Background ==\nAn interim [[Bellator Bantamweight World Championship]] bout and the final of the $1 million [[2023 in Bellator MMA#Bellator Bantamweight World Grand Prix Tournament|Bellator Bantamweight World Grand Prix Tournament]] between interim champion [[Raufeon Stots]] and former title challenger [[Patchy Mix]] headlined the event.\n\nA women's flyweight bout between former [[List of Bellator MMA champions#Women's Flyweight World Championship|Bellator Women's Flyweight Champion]] [[Ilima-Lei Macfarlane]] and [[Kana Watanabe]] was the co-main event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.khon2.com/sports/ilima-lei-macfarlane-to-face-kana-watanabe-at-bellator-295/|title=Ilima-Lei Macfarlane to face Kana Watanabe at Bellator 295|website=KHON2|author=Christian from shoulder injury vs. Otto Rodrigues at Bellator 295 |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/03/bellator-295-aaron-pico-returns-shoulder-injury-vs-otto-rodrigues-featherweight |access-date=2023-03-13 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}} However, Rodrigues had to pull out with injury in early April and was replaced by James Gonzalez.{{Cite web |last=Cruz |first=Guilherme web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2023/01/12/bellator-returning-paris-mousasi-vs-edwards-barnaoui-vs-outlaw-board/|title=Bellator Returning to Paris in May with Mousasi vs. Edwards, Barnaoui vs. Prix Tournament|Bellator Lightweight World Grand Prix]] quarterfinal bout between [[Mansour Barnaoui]] and [[Sidney Outlaw]] was scheduled to serve as the co-main event. However at the end of February, Outlaw tested positive for banned substances and was suspended, and was replaced by former [[Bellator Lightweight World Championship|Bellator Lightweight Champion]] [[Brent Primus]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/2/21/23609221/sidney-outlaw-flunks-drug-test-brent-primus-enters-lightweight-grand-prix-at-bellator-paris|title=Sidney Outlaw flunks drug test, Brent Primus enters lightweight grand prix at Bellator Paris|website=MMAFighting.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2023-02-21|access-date=2023-02-21}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[Brett Johns]] and [[Marcirley Alves]], as well a middleweight bout between Gregory Babene and Kyle Stewart were scheduled for this event.{{Cite web |date=2023-05-01 |title=Brett Johns added to Bellator 296 card in Paris {{!}} MMA UK |url=https://mmauk.net/2023/05/01/brett-johns-added-to-bellator-296-card-in-paris/ |access-date=2023-05-02 |website=mmauk.net |language=en-GB}} However, the week of the event, Johns pulled out due to [[Staphylococcal infection|staph infection]] and Stewart was not approved by the commission due to his partial [[Near-sightedness|myopia]].{{Cite news |title=Johns withdraws from Da Silva fight |language=en-GB |work=BBC Sport |url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/mixed-martial-arts/65441259 |access-date=2023-05-11}}{{Cite web |last=FightBookMMA |date=2023-05-09 |title=Bellator 296 Press Conference Quotes From The Eiffel Tower |url=https://www.fightbookmma.com/bellator-296-press-conference-quotes-from-the-eiffel-tower/ |access-date=2023-05-11 |website=FightBook MMA |language=en-US}}\n\nAt weigh-ins, Steven Hill weighed in at 174.8 pounds for his fight against Nicolo Solli \u2013 3.8 pounds over the welterweight non-title bout limit, leading to Solli to refusing to accept the bout. Romain Debienne weighed in at 172.4 lbs, 1.4 pounds over the limit, and was fined a percentage of his purse which went to his opponent.{{Cite web |last=Sherdog.com |title=Bellator 296 Weigh-in Results: Main Event Set; |language=en-US}}\n\n== Background ==\nThe event marked the promotion's fifth visit to Chicago and first since [[Bellator 288]] in November 2022.\n\nA [[List of Bellator MMA champions#Light Heavyweight World Championship|Bellator Light Heavyweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Vadim Nemkov]] and [[Yoel Romero]] headlined the event. They were previously expected to meet at [[Bellator 290]] but Nemkov withdrew due to undisclosed reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/12/27/23527933/vadim-nemkov-yoel-romero-off-bellator-290-johnny-eblen-vs-anatoly-tokov-added-as-new-co-main-event|title=Vadim Nemkov vs. Yoel Romero off Bellator 290, Johnny Eblen vs. Anatoly Tokov added as new co-main event|website=MMAFighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2022-12-27|access-date=2022-12-27}}\n\nFor the co-main event, a [[List of Bellator MMA champions#Bantamweight World Championship|Bellator Bantamweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Sergio Pettis]] and the current [[List of Bellator MMA champions#Featherweight World Championship|Bellator Featherweight Champion]] [[Patr\u00edcio Pitbull]] (also former [[List of Bellator MMA champions#Lightweight World Championship|Bellator Lightweight Champion]]) took place.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.co.uk/mma/story/_/id/35969254/patricio-pitbull-freire-fight-third-title-bellator-297|title=Patricio 'Pitbull' Freire to fight for third title at Bellator 297|website=ESPN.com|author=Jeff Wagenheim|date=2023-03-27|access-date=2023-03-28}} This was the third time in Bellator history that champions in different divisions fought for the same title, following [[2018 in Bellator MMA#Bellator 206|Bellator 206]] and [[2019 in Bellator MMA#Bellator 221|Bellator 221]] (the latter event in which Pitbull defeated [[Michael Chandler]] to become the simultaneous featherweight and lightweight champion).{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/3/27/23658273/vadim-nemkov-vs-yoel-romero-sergio-pettis-vs-patricio-pitbull-headlines-bellator-297|title=Vadim Nemkov vs. Yoel Romero, Sergio Pettis vs. Patricio Pitbull headlines Bellator 297|website=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2023-03-27|access-date=2023-03-28}}\n\nIf successful, Pitbull would have become the first man to become a three division champion in a major MMA organization, the first man to become champion in two divisions simultaneously twice, and that his win would have mark the fourth time overall where a fighter has won a title in different divisions (after [[Joe Warren (fighter)|Joe Warren]] at [[2014 in Bellator MMA#Bellator 128|Bellator 128]], [[Ryan Bader]] at [[2019 in Bellator MMA#Bellator 214|Bellator 214]], and Pitbull at Bellator 221).{{Cite web |date=2023-06-13 |title=Bellator 297 pre-event facts: Patricio 'Pitbull' can be first to achieve triple champ status |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/bellator-297-pre-event-facts-patricio-freire-history-three-titles-sergio-pettis-vadim-nemkov-yoel-romero |access-date=2023-06-13 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}} Meanwhile,\nPitbull also became the first challenger in a Bellator champion vs. champion bout to drop a division.{{Cite web |date=2023-03-28 |title=Bellator 297 heads to Chicago with Vadim Nemkov vs. Yoel Romero, Sergio Pettis vs. Patricio Pitbull |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/03/bellator-297-mma-news-vadim-nemkov-vs-yoel-romero-sergio-pettis-vs-patricio-pitbull-booked |access-date=2023-03-28 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}}\n\nA welterweight bout between [[Bobby Nash]] and Roman Faraldo was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web |last=FightBookMMA |date=2023-04-12 |title=BELLATOR 297: Nemkov vs. Romero adds 8 more fights |url=https://www.fightbookmma.com/bellator-297-nemkov-vs-romero-adds-8-more-fights/ |access-date=2023-04-12 |website=FightBook MMA |language=en-US}} In the beginning of May, it was announced that Faraldo had pulled out of the bout and replaced by [[Kyle Crutchmer]].{{Cite web |last=Sherdog.com |title=Austin Vanderford vs. Imamshafi Aliev, Six Other Bouts Complete Bellator 297 Lineup |url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Austin-Vanderford-vs-Imamshafi-Aliev-Six-Other-Bouts-Complete-Bellator-297-Lineup-189892 |access-date=2023-05-03 |website=Sherdog |language=en}}\n\nA middleweight bout between [[Austin Vanderford]] and Imamshafi Aliev and a bantamweight bout between [[Leandro Higo]] and Nikita Mikhailov were scheduled for this event. However, both bouts were scrapped for unknown reasons for the middleweight bout and illness for Mikhailov for the bantamweight one.{{Cite web |last=King |first=Nolan |date=2023-06-10 |title=Bellator the event. The pairing had previously met at [[2017 in Bellator MMA#Bellator 186|Bellator 186]] in a [[List of Bellator MMA champions#Light Heavyweight World Championship|Bellator Light Heavyweight World Championship]] bout in November 2017, where defending champion Bader won by second-round TKO. However, Vassell withdrew due to illness and the bout was cancelled.{{Cite web |last=Wagenheim |first=Jeff |date=2023-10-03 |title=Linton Vassell out of Bellator 300 title bout due to illness |url=https://www.espn.com.au/mma/story/_/id/38548406/linton-vassell-bellator-300-title-bout-due-illness |access-date=2023-10-03 |website=ESPN |language=en-US}} As a result, a [[2023 in Bellator MMA#Bellator Lightweight World Grand Prix Tournament|Bellator Lightweight World Grand Prix]] semifinal bout for the [[Bellator Lightweight World Championship]] between current champion [[Usman Nurmagomedov]] and former champion [[Brent Primus]] that was already scheduled to be on the card was promoted to be the new main event.{{Cite web |last=Guillen Jr |first=Adam |date=2023-08-09 |title=Bellator 300 set with four title fights featuring Ryan Bader, Cris Cyborg and Usman Nurmagomedov |url=https://www.mmamania.com/2023/8/9/23826217/bellator-300-four-title-fights-ryan-bader-cris-cyborg-usman-nurmagomedov-showtime-mma |access-date=2023-09-13 |website=SBNation |language=en-US}}\n\nA [[List of Bellator MMA champions#Women's Featherweight World Championship|Bellator Women's Featherweight World Championship]] bout between current champion [[Cris Cyborg]] (also former [[List of Strikeforce champions#Women's Featherweight Championship|Strikeforce]], [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Featherweight Championship|Invicta FC]] and [[UFC Women's Featherweight Championship|UFC Women's Featherweight Champions]]) and [[Cat Zingano]] served as the co-main event.\n\nA [[List of Bellator MMA champions#Women's Flyweight World Championship|Bellator Women's Flyweight World Championship]] bout between current champion [[Liz Carmouche]] and former champion [[Ilima-Lei Macfarlane]] completed the triad of title fights. However at the weigh-ins, Macfarlane came in at 126.6 lb (after coming in at 127.4 lb in her first attempt), 1.6 pounds over the women's flyweight limit for a title fight. The commission gave her an additional hour to cut weight, but Macfarlane came in at 128.2 lb. As a result, the bout will proceed as a one way title bout, where in the event that Macfarlane wins the fight, she would be ineligible for the championship and the title would be left vacant, while if Carmouche wins it would still count as a title defense.{{Cite web |date=2023-10-06 |title=Bellator 300 weigh-in results: Ilima-Lei Macfarlane misses weight, can't win title from champ Liz Carmouche |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/10/bellator-300-san-diego-cyborg-zingano-carmouche-mcfarlane-nurmagomedov-primus-weigh-in-results-live-stream |access-date=2023-10-07 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}}\n\n== Results ==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Showtime/Paramount+)}}\n{{MMAevent bout|Lightweight|[[Usman Nurmagomedov]] (c)|vs|[[Brent Primus]]|NC (overturned)|5|5:00|[[2023 in Bellator MMA#Bellator Lightweight World Grand Prix Tournament|Bellator Lightweight World Grand Prix]] Semifinal for the [[Bellator Lightweight World Championship]]; originally a unanimous decision win for Nurmagomedov, overturned after he tested positive for a banned substance.\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout|Women's Featherweight|[[Cris Cyborg]] (c)|def.|[[Cat Zingano]]|TKO (punches)|1|4:01|For the [[Bellator Women's Featherweight World Championship]].\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout|Women's Flyweight|[[Liz Carmouche]] (c)|def.|[[Ilima-Lei Macfarlane]]|TKO (leg kick)|5|0:17|For the [[Bellator Women's Flyweight World Championship]]. officially acquires Bellator MMA, reveals plans for champs vs. champs card in 2024|publisher=mmafighting.com}}\n\n== Background ==\nThe event marked the promotion's sixth visit to Chicago and the second in 2023 since [[Bellator 297]] in June.\n\nA [[List of Bellator champions#Welterweight World Championship|Bellator Welterweight World Championship]] bout between current champion [[Yaroslav Amosov]] and former [[Legacy Fighting Alliance#LFA Welterweight Championship|LFA Welterweight Champion]] [[Jason Jackson (fighter)|Jason Jackson]] headlined the event.\n\nA [[List of Bellator MMA champions#Bantamweight World Championship|Bellator Bantamweight World Championship]] unification bout between current champion [[Sergio Pettis]] and interim champion [[Patchy Mix]] (also [[2023 in Bellator MMA#Bellator Bantamweight World Grand Prix Tournament|Bellator Bantamweight World Grand Prix]] winner) served as the co-main event.\n\nA [[2023 in Bellator MMA#Bellator Lightweight World Grand Prix Tournament|Bellator Lightweight World Grand Prix]] semifinal bout between former champion [[Patricky Pitbull]] and [[Alexandr Shabliy]] took place at the event.\n\nA bantamweight rematch between [[Raufeon Stots]] and [[Danny Sabatello]] also took place at the event. The pairing previously met at [[Bellator 289]] in December 2022, which Stots won via split decision.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com.au/mma/story/_/id/35222197/raufeon-stots-edges-danny-sabatello-gets-patchy-mix-bellator-final?platform=amp|title=Raufeon Stots edges Danny Sabatello, gets Patchy Mix in Bellator final|publisher=ESPN|author=Jeff Wagenheim|date=2022-12-10|access-date=2023-09-14}}\n\nThe week of the event, the women's flyweight bout between [[Juliana Velasquez]] and Paula Cristina and a heavyweight bout between [[Daniel James (fighter)|Daniel James]] and [[Ali Isaev]] were cancelled.{{Cite web |last=Guillen |first=Adam |date=2023-11-13 |title=Bellator 301 loses not one, but two fights on the same day |url=https://www.mmamania.com/2023/11/13/23959844/bellator-301-loses-two-fights-on-the-same-day-velasquez-james-pitbull-mckee-amosov-mma |access-date=2023-11-14 |website=MMAmania.com |language=en}}\n\nAt weigh ins, Keri Taylor-Melendez weighed in at 126.6 pounds, .6 pounds over the flyweight limit. The bout proceeded at catchweight and Taylor-Melendez was fined a percentage of her purse which went to Seng\u00fcl.{{Cite web |date=2023-11-16 |title=Bellator 301 weigh-in results: Fighters on stacked main card hit marks in Chicago |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/11/bellator-301-chicago-aj-mckee-patricky-pitbull-pettis-patchy-mix-sabatello-stots-amosov-jackson-weigh-in-results-video |access-date=2023-11-18 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between Tyrell Fortune and [[Marcelo Golm]] was planned for the event. Despite both men weighing in, the bout was cancelled the night of the event after Fourtune became ill backstage.{{Cite web |last=Anderson |first=Jay |date=2023-11-17 |title=Tyrell Fortune vs. Marcelo Golm Drops Off Bellator 301 With Card Underway |url=https://cagesidepress.com/2023/11/17/tyrell-fortune-marcelo-golm-drops-off-bellator-301/ |access-date=2023-11-17 |website=cagesidepress.com |language=en}}\n\n== Results ==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Showtime)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Welterweight\n|[[Jason Jackson (fighter)|Jason Jackson]]\n|def.\n|[[Yaroslav Amosov]] (c)\n|KO (punches)\n|3\n|2:08\n|For the [[Bellator Welterweight World Championship]]\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Bantamweight\n|[[Patchy Mix]] (ic)\n|def.\n|[[Sergio Pettis]] (c)\n|Submission (rear-naked choke)\n|2\n|1:51\n|For the [[Bellator Bantamweight World Championship]].\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Bantamweight\n|[[Raufeon Stots]]\n|def.\n|[[Danny Sabatello]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (29\u201328, 29\u201328, 29\u201328)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|[[A. vs. Bellator: Champions]] on February 24.{{Cite web |date=2024-01-17 |title=Vadim Nemkov officially vacates Bellator light heavyweight title J. McKee]], [[Juan Archuleta]] and [[Kyoji Horiguchi]]. Matches were contested under Rizin's ruleset, which differs from Bellator's usual [[Unified Rules of Mixed Martial Arts]].{{Cite web|author=Yanofsky, Daniel|url=https://www.sportingnews.com/us/mma/news/rizin-rules-explained-mma-bellator-ufc/afeapufwt0mznivhsp4nehsa|title=RIZIN rules, explained: Comparing MMA organization to Bellator, UFC|publisher=[[Sporting News]]|date=2022-12-29|access-date=2022-12-31}}\n\nThe main card aired tape delayed in the U.S. on [[Showtime (TV network)|Showtime]], Bellator's regular broadcast partner.\n\n== Results ==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Bellator MMA vs. Rizin (Showtime)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|[[A. Background ==\nThe event marked the second co-promotion between Bellator MMA and the Rizin Fighting Federation, and Bellator's third visit to [[Saitama (city)|Saitama]]; following [[Bellator MMA vs. Rizin]] in December 2022, and [[2019 in Bellator MMA#Bellator 237|Bellator 237]] on December 29, 2019.{{Cite web |last=Raimondi |first=Marc |date=2023-05-27 |title=AJ McKee vs. Patricky Freire headlines Bellator-Rizin event|url=https://www.espn.in/mma/story/_/id/37738956/aj-mckee-vs-patricky-freire-headlines-bellator-rizin-event?platform=amp|access-date=2023-06-01 |website=ESPN |language=en}}\n\nA [[2023 in Bellator MMA#Bellator Lightweight World Grand Prix Tournament|Bellator Lightweight World Grand Prix]] quarterfinal bout between former [[List of Bellator MMA champions#Featherweight World Championship|Bellator Featherweight Champion]] [[A. J. McKee]] and former [[List of Bellator MMA champions#Lightweight World Championship|Bellator Lightweight Champion]] [[Patricky Pitbull]] was scheduled to headline the event. However, on July 26, it was announced that McKee had pulled out of the bout due to medical issues and was replaced by the incumbent [[Rizin Fighting Federation#Lightweight Championship|Rizin Lightweight Champion]] [[Roberto de Souza]] at a catchweight of 161 pounds.{{cite web |last1=Wells |first1=Matthew |title=Major changes to Bellator X Rizin 2: A.J. McKee out; Patricky Freire faces RIZIN champ; Patricio 'Pitbull' added |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/07/bellator-x-rizin-2-news-aj-mckee-out-patricky-freire-vs-roberto-de-souza-patricio-freire-vs-chihiro-suzuki |website=[[MMA Junkie]]|date=July 26, 2023 |access-date=July 26, 2023}}\n\nFor the co-main event, an inaugural [[List of Bellator MMA champions#Flyweight World Championship|Bellator Flyweight Championship]] bout between former [[List of Bellator MMA champions#Bantamweight World Championship|Bellator Bantamweight Champion]] [[Kyoji Horiguchi]] (also former two-time [[Rizin Fighting Federation#Bantamweight Championship|Rizin Bantamweight Champion]]) and Makoto Takahashi took place at the event.{{Cite web |last=Martin |first=Damon |date=2023-05-27 |title=AJ McKee vs. Patricky Pitbull, first ever Bellator flyweight champion will be crowned at Bellator x RIZIN 2 in July|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/5/27/23739503/aj-mckee-vs-patricky-pitbull-first-bellator-flyweight-champion-crowned-at-bellator-x-rizin-2-in-july|access-date=2023-06-01 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en}} If successful, Horiguchi would have become the fourth fighter in Bellator history to win a title in different divisions (after [[Joe Warren (fighter)|Joe Warren]] at [[2014 in Bellator MMA#Bellator 128|Bellator 128]], [[Ryan Bader]] at [[2019 in Bellator MMA#Bellator 214|Bellator 214]], and [[Patr\u00edcio Pitbull]] at [[2019 in Bellator MMA#Bellator 221|Bellator 221]]).\n\nA [[Rizin Fighting Federation#Bantamweight Championship|Rizin Bantamweight Championship]] bout for the vacant title between former [[List of Bellator MMA champions#Bantamweight World Championship|Bellator Bantamweight Champion]] [[Juan Archuleta]] and former champion [[Kai Asakura]] was scheduled to take place at the event.{{Cite web |last=Riggs |first=Drake |date=2023-05-30 |title=Juan Archuleta 'couldn't ask for a better position' between Bellator and RIZIN as he pursues gold in Japan|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2023/5/30/23739549/juan-archuleta-couldnt-ask-for-a-better-position-between-bellator-and-rizin-as-he-pursues-gold-japan|access-date=2023-06-01 |website=MMA Mania |language=en}} Former champion Kyoji Horiguchi vacated the title on December 22 after moving down to the flyweight division.{{Cite web |last=Martin |first=Damon |date=2022-12-28 |title=Kyoji Horiguchi pushes Bellator to add 125-pound division: 'Flyweight is my weight class'|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/12/28/23529676/kyoji-horiguchi-pushes-bellator-to-add-125-pound-division-flyweight-is-my-weight-class |access-date=2023-06-01 |website=MMA Fighting |language=en}} However, on July 18 it was announced that Asakura withdrew due to injury and was replaced by [[Hiromasa Ougikubo]].{{Cite web|title=Ougikubo Replaces Asakura, Faces Archuleta for Bantamweight Title at Super Rizin 2 |url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Ougikubo-Replaces-Asakura-Faces-Archuleta-for-Bantamweight-Title-at-Super-Rizin-2-190529 |website=sherdog.com |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/1/16/24040259/pfl-vs-bellator-fight-card-set-for-feb-24-in-saudi-arabia-four-champ-vs-champ-fights-scheduled |access-date=2024-01-16 |website=MMA Fighting |language=en-CA}}\n\n== Background ==\nIn November 2023, it was announced that the PFL had acquired rival promotion [[Bellator MMA]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/11/pfl-bellator-acquistion-ufc-sale-promotions-merger-future-plans|title=PFL officially announces Bellator acquisition, plans revealed for future|publisher=MMA Junkie|author=Nolan King|date=2023-11-20|access-date=2023-11-20}} Speculation was that the company would run a champion against champion event in 2024, bolstered by appearances of Bellator MMA champions at PFL's final event of 2023.{{cite news |date=20 November 2023 |title=Professional Fighters League acquires rival MMA promotion Bellator in 'transformative deal' |url=https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/mixed-martial-arts/67478710 |access-date=28 September 2026 |work=BBC Sport}}\n\nA champion versus champion card was announced on January 16, 2024, and scheduled for the following month. Originally, the event was to see four champions from four divisions (heavyweight, middleweight, welterweight, and light heavyweight) defend their titles from their respective organizations.{{Cite web |date=2024-01-16 |title=Bellator MMA vs. PFL supercard: Four champion vs. champion fights set for February in Saudi Arabia |url=https://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/bellator-mma-vs-pfl-supercard-four-champion-vs-champion-fights-set-for-february-in-saudi-arabia/ |access-date=2024-01-16 |website=CBSSports.com |language=en}} However, two champion versus champion matches were altered leading up to the card:\n\n*[[2018 in Professional Fighters League#2018 PFL Welterweight playoffs|2018]] and [[2023 in Professional Fighters League#2023 PFL Welterweight playoffs|2023 PFL welterweight]] winner [[Magomed Magomedkerimov]] was originally announced to face the [[Bellator Welterweight Champion]] (also former [[Legacy Fighting Alliance#LFA Welterweight Championship|LFA Welterweight Champion]]) [[Jason Jackson (fighter)|Jason Jackson]] at this event. However, Magomedkerimov withdrew from the bout due to injury in January 2024 and was replaced by [[2019 in Professional Fighters League#2019 PFL Welterweight playoffs|2019]] and [[2021 in Professional Fighters League#2021 PFL Welterweight playoffs|2021 PFL welterweight]] winner [[Ray Cooper III]] in a 180 pounds catchweight bout.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/01/pfl-vs-bellator-fight-card-ray-cooper-vs-magomed-magomedkerimov-jason-jackson|title=Ray Cooper III replaces Magomed Magomedkerimov at PFL vs. Bellator event, faces Jason Jackson|publisher=MMA Junkie|author=Nolan King|date=2024-01-22|access-date=2024-01-22}}\n*A featherweight bout between reigning [[Bellator Featherweight Champion]] [[Patr\u00edcio Pitbull]] and [[2023 in Professional Fighters League#2023 PFL Featherweight playoffs|2023 PFL featherweight]] winner [[Jesus Pinedo]] was scheduled for this event; however, Pinedo pulled out a week before the event with a back injury.{{Cite web |last=Lee |first=Alexander K. |date=2024-02-17 |title=Patricio Pitbull vs. Jesus Pinedo off PFL vs. Bellator card following Pinedo withdrawal Pitbull was instead booked against [[2023 in Professional Fighters League#2023 PFL Featherweight playoffs|2023 PFL featherweight]] finalist Gabriel Alves Braga who was already on the card, while Braga's opponent, [[Aaron Pico]], was booked in a rematch against [[Henry Corrales]].{{Cite web |last=Newswire |first=MMA Fighting |date=2024-02-19 |title=Gabriel Braga vs. Patricio Pitbull set for PFL vs. Bellator, Aaron Pico expected to stay on card |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/2/19/24077872/gabriel-braga-vs-patricio-pitbull-set-for-pfl-vs-bellator-aaron-pico-expected-to-stay-on-card |title=Patricio Pitbull vs. Gabriel Braga off PFL vs. Bellator card |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/2/23/24081103/patricio-pitbull-gabriel-braga-off-pfl-bellator-card |access-date=2024-02-24 |website=MMA Fighting |language=en}}\n\nThe undercard also included bouts between fighters from the rival promotions, with the following fights being added as well:\n* A heavyweight bout between [[2021 in Professional Fighters League#2021 PFL Heavyweight playoffs|2021 PFL heavyweight]] winner [[Bruno Cappelozza]] and former [[Bellator Light Heavyweight Champion]] (also the [[2021 in Bellator MMA#Bellator Light Heavyweight World Grand Prix Tournament|Bellator Light Heavyweight World Grand Prix]] winner) [[Vadim Nemkov]].\n*A light heavyweight bout between [[Thiago Santos]] and [[Yoel Romero]].\n*A lightweight bout between [[Clay Collard]] and former [[Bellator Featherweight Champion]] (also the [[2021 in Bellator MMA#Bellator Featherweight World Grand Prix Tournament|Bellator Featherweight World Grand Prix]] winner) [[A. J. McKee]].\n*A featherweight bout between [[Aaron Pico]] and [[2023 in Professional Fighters League#2023 PFL Featherweight playoffs|2023 PFL featherweight]] finalist Gabriel Alves Braga.\nIn addition, the card featured the MMA return of [[Claressa Shields]] and the professional MMA debut of Biaggio Ali Walsh, the grandson of [[Muhammad Ali]] who previously competed for PFL in amateur bouts. The bout between Claressa Shields and Kelsey De Santis, which Shields won, was the first professional MMA bout in Saudi Arabia featuring two women.{{Cite web|url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/mixed-martial-arts/68394606|title=MMA: Claressa Shields beats Kelsey De Santis to make history in Saudi Arabia|date=February 24, 2024|via=www.bbc.com}}\n\nUFC heavyweight champion [[Jon Jones]] made an appearance on the broadcast.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2024/2/24/24082374/sorry-jon-jones-crashes-historic-pfl-broadcast-rooting-for-francis-ngannou-bellator-ufc-mma-espn|title=Sorry!
